```
def line_objective(alpha, c0, c1, cross, n):
    a=alpha; b=1-a
    c = b*b*c0 + 2*a*b*cross + a*a*c1
    return 2*n*float(np.max(c))
```

Function `build_convolve_matrix(f, n, double=False)`:
A shape (2n-1, n), A[k,i]=f[k-i] if 0<=k-i<n else 0. We can fill efficiently:
For k in range(R): s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k); A[k,s:e+1] = f[k-(s:e+1)] = f[k-s : k-e]. That slice length e-s+1. Since f[k-s: k-e+1]? If i=s..e, j=k-i runs k-s down to k-e. We need f[j] in i order, so segment reversed: f[k-s : k-e-1 : -1]? Actually j = k-i, when i increasing, j decreasing. But matrix column i should get f[k-i]. We can assign A[k, s:e+1] = f[k-s : k-e-1 : -1]? Let's test k=3,n=5: s=0,e=3, j=3,2,1,0. f[3:-1:-1]? In Python f[3:-1:-1] gives f[3],f[2],f[1]? Stop -1 exclusive so stops at1? Better use negative? Simpler loop over i slice: `A[k, s:e+1] = f[k - np.arange(s, e+1)]` O(total) creates arrays. Fine for n up to 2048, total assignments ~n^2; can do.
For SLP B=2*A.

But building dense for many LPs expensive; can cache? f changes each iteration. Maybe okay. For n=512, R=1023,n=512, 523k entries, building ~0.005s? Python loops 1023 slicing maybe 0.01. For 1024 1M, okay. For 2048 4M, maybe0.1.

Best-response LP (primal) might be more stable. Let's implement both and choose best candidate.

Primal best-response LP:
Variables g (n) + t. Minimize t. Constraints A_f g - t ≤0, sum g=1. Bounds g∈[0,1], t∈[0,1]. A same as build (f). Use linprog. g gives candidate. This LP doesn't depend on c0. It may find g that minimizes cross with f. We can line search f to g.

Dual max-min LP variables μ (R)+s. Maximize s: constraints -A^T μ + s 1 ≤0, sum μ=1. Bounds μ≥0,s≥0. Variables R+1 could be more, but for n=512 R=1023, similar. It gives μ. We can use μ for multiplicative soft update. But solving both primal and dual plus SLP maybe too many LPs. We can choose adaptively: SLP and primal best-response likely enough.

```
lo=0; hi=1
br=0.618
while hi-lo>1e-6:
    m1=lo+ (1-br)*(hi-lo); m2=lo+br*(hi-lo)
    if f(m1)<f(m2): hi=m2 else lo=m1
alpha=(lo+hi)/2
```

```
        # multiplicative polish with soft peaks
        for ...: multiplicative update
        if stagnation break
```

Let's write core functions:

`normalize_to_sum1(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray(a,float)
a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
if not finite: return None
s=a.sum()
if s<=1e-300: return None
return a/s
```

```
_LBJAX = None? 
def smooth_objective(z):
   tau=_SMOOTH_TAU; n=_SMOOTH_N
   m=np.max(z); ez=np.exp(z-m); p=ez/ez.sum()
   c=np.convolve(p,p)
   mx=np.max(c)
   # stable logsumexp
   vals=c-mx
   smax=vals.max()
   s=np.sum(np.exp(vals-smax))
   F=mx+smax+np.log(s)
   val=2*n*F
   return val
```

```
   # variables μ,s

```

`optimize_length(n, budget, seed, parent_arr, initial)`:
- If initial None, create starts and choose best after quick multiplicative.
- Then run a loop for budget:
   - Alternate: soft multiplicative for e.g. 10-30s, SLP LP for 1 line search, best-response LP if time, soft multiplicative.
   - Use failures to break.
Pseudo:
```
def optimize_length(n,budget,seed,parent):
  best=initial or best of starts
  bestsc=score
  t0; deadline=t0+budget
  rng=...
  iter=0
  while time<deadline:
     rem=deadline-time
     if rem<2: break
     # choose action
     if iter % 3 ==0 and rem>20 and n<=1536:
        # SLP
        cand = slp_step(best)
        if cand: score; update
     elif iter %3==1 and rem>20 and n<=1536:
        cand = best_response_step(best)
     else:
        # multiplicative batch limited e.g. min(30, rem/2)
        best,sc = multiplicative_optimize_soft(best, min(20, rem/2), seed)
     # stagnation reset: perturb best
     iter+=1
  return best,bestsc
```

`multiplicative_optimize_soft` parameters: `max_time`, `seed`. It should not use more than max_time. It uses current best sequence. We'll pass `budget` perhaps 10-60s.

Pseudo:
```
def multiplicative_optimize_soft(p, max_time, seed):
    best=normalize(p); bestsc=score_norm(best)
    if max_time<=0: return best,bestsc
    t0=...; deadline=t0+max_time
    temps=[0.0,0.02,0.05,0.1,0.2,0.5,1.0]
    cur=best.copy(); cursc=bestsc
    eta=0.35; temp_idx=int(seed % len(temps)); stuck=0; best_stuck=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       temp=temps[temp_idx]
       cand, sc = smooth_one_step(cur, eta, temp)
       if sc < cursc:
          cur=cand; cursc=sc
       else:
          stuck +=1
          if stuck>40:
             cur=best.copy(); cursc=bestsc; stuck=0
             temp_idx=(temp_idx+1)%len(temps)
             eta=0.35
       if cursc < bestsc:
          best=cur; bestsc=cursc; best_stuck=0
          if eta<0.8: eta*=1.03
       else:
          best_stuck+=1
          if best_stuck>250:
             # noise
             noise=np.exp(np.random.default_rng(seed+it).normal(0,0.07,cur.size))
             cur=normalize(cur*noise); cursc=score_norm(cur)
             eta=0.35; temp_idx=(temp_idx+1)%len(temps); best_stuck=0
       if time >= deadline break
       it+=1
    return best,bestsc
```
`smooth_one_step` with scale line search:
```
def smooth_one_step(p, eta, temp_frac):
   c=np.convolve(p,p); M=max
   if temp_frac <=0:
      W=np.zeros_like(c)
      active=c >= M*(1-1e-7)
      if not active.any(): active np.argmax top
      W[active]=1/active_count
   else:
      temp=max(temp_frac*M,1e-15)
      W=np.exp((c-M)/temp); W/=sum
   h=cross_weights(W,p)
   if not finite or max<=0: return p.copy(),score_norm(p)
   d=-(h - np.mean(h))/max(h)
   # multipliers
   bestcand=p; bestsc=score_norm(p)
   for gamma in (eta*0.25, eta*0.5, eta, eta*1.5):
      fac=np.clip(gamma*d, -5,5)
      cand=normalize(p*np.exp(fac))
      if cand is None: continue
      sc=score_norm(cand)
      if sc<bestsc: bestcand=cand; bestsc=sc
   return bestcand,bestsc
```
This evaluates up to4 convolutions per step. For 10s, maybe 100 steps? n=512 conv cheap. Could reduce to 3 scales [eta*0.5, eta, eta*1.5]. We'll use [eta*0.5, eta, eta*2.0]. For eta small. To save, if temp hard active and p flat, maybe. Use current cur only. If none improve, stuck. Good.

```
           a=c*np.exp(th*x)
           a=normalize_to_sum1(a); if a: starts.append
       # waves
       i=np.arange(n)
       for f,amp in [(1,0.5),(2,0.4),(3,0.3)]:
           a=c*(1+amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*f*i/n + th_phase?))
```

```
def line_objective(alpha, c0, c1, cross, n):
    a=alpha; b=1.0-a
    c = b*b*c0 + 2.0*a*b*cross + a*a*c1
```

```
def get_active_indices(c, n, frac=0.1):
   M=float(np.max(c))
   if M<=0: return np.array([int(np.argmax(c))])
   thresh=M*(1-frac)
   act=np.nonzero(c >= thresh)[0]
   K=max(64, n//4)
   if K < c.size:
       top=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
       act=np.unique(np.concatenate((act, top)))
   return act
```
For central, c first n exactly M; due floating maybe equal. If n=512, act size up to 512. Good.

`get_fft_n(n)`:
```
def get_fft_n(n):
    val = _FFT_N_CACHE.get(n, None)
    if val is not None: return val
    N=1
    need=2*n -1
    while N < need: N <<=1
    _FFT_N_CACHE[n]=N
    return N
```

```
h=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conj(fp), N)
h=h[:n].real
return np.maximum(h,0.0)
```

```python
"""
Search for low-score nonnegative step-function coefficients.

The score is
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)**2,
which is scale-invariant.  This program searches over several lengths and
uses:
  - analytically motivated central-binomial / power-law starts,
  - soft exponentiated-gradient multiplicative updates using FFT cross
    correlations,
  - successive linear-programming (SLP) cuts on the active convolution peaks,
  - a convex best-response LP that minimizes max cross-convolution,
  - line searches on convex mixing paths,
  - a final polish from the best sequence found.
"""

import time
import numpy as np

_HAVE_SCIPY = False
try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

_FFT_N_CACHE = {}


def get_fft_n(n):
    val = _FFT_N_CACHE.get(n, None)
    if val is not None:
        return val
    N = 1
    need = 2 * n - 1
    while N < need:
        N <<= 1
    _FFT_N_CACHE[n] = N
    return N


def convolve_full(a, b=None):
    if b is None:
        b = a
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    R = 2 * n - 1
    if n <= 768:
        return np.convolve(a, b)
    N = get_fft_n(n)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, N)
    return c[:R]


def cross_weights(w, p):
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    N = get_fft_n(n)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, N)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conj(fp), N)
    h = np.maximum(h[:n].real, 0.0)
    return h


def normalize_to_sum1(a):
    if a is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return None
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a = a / s
    if not np.isfinite(a).all():
        return None
    return a


def local_score_norm(p):
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = convolve_full(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m)


def evaluate_normalized(seq):
    a = normalize_to_sum1(seq)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    sc = local_score_norm(a)
    return a, sc


def try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand):
    seq, sc = evaluate_normalized(cand)
    if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        return seq, sc, True
    return best_seq, best_score, False


def make_central(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(n - 1):
        a[i + 1] = a[i] * (2.0 * i + 1.0) / (2.0 * (i + 1.0))
    return normalize_to_sum1(a)


def interpolate_parent_to_length(parent, n):
    if parent is None or n <= 0:
        return None
    try:
        p = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if p.size < 2:
        return None
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.isfinite(p).all():
        return None
    xs = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, p.size)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return np.interp(xn, xs, p)


def make_starts(n, parent, seed):
    starts = []
    if n <= 0:
        return starts

    c = make_central(n)
    if c is not None:
        starts.append(c)
        starts.append(c[::-1])

        i = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64)
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        for th in (-1.5, -0.75, 0.75, 1.5, 2.5):
            starts.append(c * np.exp(th * x))
        for fr, amp in ((1, 0.45), (2, 0.35), (3, 0.25)):
            starts.append(c * (1.0 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * fr * i / float(n))))

    j = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    for alpha in (0.30, 0.50, 0.70):
        starts.append(j ** (-alpha))
        starts.append((j ** (-alpha))[::-1])

    d = np.minimum(j, n - j + 1.0)
    starts.append(1.0 / np.sqrt(d))
    starts.append(1.0 / d)

    starts.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64))

    if parent is not None:
        starts.append(interpolate_parent_to_length(parent, n))

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + 100003 + int(n) * 9176)
    starts.append(rng.beta(2.0, 2.0, n))

    v = rng.normal(size=n)
    w = max(3, min(9, n))
    v = np.convolve(v, np.ones(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(w), mode="same")
    v = np.clip(v, 0.0, None)
    starts.append(v)
    starts.append(v[::-1])

    out = []
    for s in starts:
        a = normalize_to_sum1(s)
        if a is not None:
            out.append(a)
    return out


def smooth_one_step(p, eta, temp_frac):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")
    base_sc = local_score_norm(p)
    c = convolve_full(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return p.copy(), base_sc
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return p.copy(), base_sc

    if temp_frac <= 1e-12:
        thresh = M * (1.0 - 1e-7)
        act = np.flatnonzero(c >= thresh)
        if act.size == 0:
            K = max(1, min(c.size, 64))
            act = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        W = np.zeros_like(c)
        W[act] = 1.0 / float(act.size)
    else:
        temp = max(float(temp_frac) * M, 1e-15)
        W = np.exp((c - M) / temp)
        ws = float(np.sum(W))
        if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
            return p.copy(), base_sc
        W /= ws

    h = cross_weights(W, p)
    hmax = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(hmax) or hmax <= 0.0:
        return p.copy(), base_sc

    d = -(h - float(np.mean(h))) / hmax
    best_cand = p.copy()
    best_sc = base_sc

    for gamma in (0.5 * eta, eta, 2.0 * eta):
        fac = np.clip(gamma * d, -6.0, 6.0)
        cand = normalize_to_sum1(p * np.exp(fac))
        if cand is None:
            continue
        sc = local_score_norm(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_cand = cand
            best_sc = sc
    return best_cand, best_sc


def multiplicative_optimize_soft(p, max_time, seed):
    seq, sc = evaluate_normalized(p)
    if seq is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    best = seq
    bestsc = sc
    if max_time <= 0.0:
        return best, bestsc

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(max_time)

    temps = [0.0, 0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.50, 1.00]
    cur = best.copy()
    cursc = bestsc
    eta = 0.35
    tidx = int(abs(seed % 1000)) % len(temps)
    stall = 0
    best_stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        temp = temps[tidx]
        cand, sc = smooth_one_step(cur, eta, temp)
        if cand is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < cursc:
                cur = cand
                cursc = sc
            else:
                stall += 1
                if stall > 35:
                    cur = best.copy()
                    cursc = bestsc
                    stall = 0
                    tidx = (tidx + 1) % len(temps)
                    eta = 0.35

            seq, sc2 = evaluate_normalized(cur)
            if seq is not None and sc2 < bestsc:
                best = seq
                bestsc = sc2
                best_stall = 0
                if eta < 0.85:
                    eta = float(np.clip(eta * 1.03, 0.05, 0.85))
            else:
                best_stall += 1
                if best_stall > 180:
                    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) + it * 101 + 7)
                    noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.08, cur.size))
                    cur = normalize_to_sum1(cur * noise)
                    if cur is None:
                        cur = best.copy()
                    cursc = local_score_norm(cur)
                    eta = 0.35
                    tidx = (tidx + 1) % len(temps)
                    best_stall = 0
        else:
            tidx = (tidx + 1) % len(temps)
            eta = 0.35
            stall = 0

        it += 1

    return best, bestsc


def line_objective(alpha, c0, c1, cross, n):
    a = float(alpha)
    b = 1.0 - a
    c = b * b * c0 + 2.0 * a * b * cross + a * a * c1
    return float(2.0 * n * np.max(c))


def best_alpha_line(p0, p1):
    p0 = normalize_to_sum1(p0)
    p1 = normalize_to_sum1(p1)
    if p0 is None or p1 is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(p0.size)
    c0 = convolve_full(p0, p0)
    c1 = convolve_full(p1, p1)
    cross = convolve_full(p0, p1)

    alphas = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 65)
    best_idx = 0
    best_val = line_objective(0.0, c0, c1, cross, n)
    for idx in range(1, alphas.size):
        v = line_objective(alphas[idx], c0, c1, cross, n)
        if v < best_val:
            best_val = v
            best_idx = idx

    if best_idx == 0:
        lo = 0.0
        hi = alphas[2]
    elif best_idx == alphas.size - 1:
        lo = alphas[-2]
        hi = 1.0
    else:
        lo = alphas[best_idx - 1]
        hi = alphas[best_idx + 1]

    for _ in range(45):
        m1 = lo + (hi - lo) / 3.0
        m2 = lo + 2.0 * (hi - lo) / 3.0
        v1 = line_objective(m1, c0, c1, cross, n)
        v2 = line_objective(m2, c0, c1, cross, n)
        if v1 < v2:
            hi = m2
        else:
            lo = m1
    alpha = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
    best_val = line_objective(alpha, c0, c1, cross, n)

    cand = normalize_to_sum1((1.0 - alpha) * p0 + alpha * p1)
    if cand is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    return cand, best_val


def get_active_indices(c, n):
    M = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return np.array([0], dtype=np.int64)
    thresh = M * (1.0 - 0.08)
    act = np.flatnonzero(c >= thresh)
    K = max(64, n // 4)
    K = min(K, c.size)
    if K < c.size:
        top = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
        act = np.unique(np.concatenate((act, top)))
    if act.size == 0:
        act = np.array([int(np.argmax(c))], dtype=np.int64)
    return act


def build_slp_aub(p, active, c0):
    n = int(p.size)
    R = int(active.size)
    A_ub = np.zeros((R, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for idx, k in enumerate(active):
        k = int(k)
        s = k - (n - 1)
        if s < 0:
            s = 0
        e = k
        if e >= n:
            e = n - 1
        if s <= e:
            start = k - s
            stop = k - e
            if stop == 0:
                A_ub[idx, s:e + 1] = 2.0 * p[start::-1]
            else:
                A_ub[idx, s:e + 1] = 2.0 * p[start:stop - 1:-1]
        A_ub[idx, n] = -1.0
    b_ub = c0[active].copy()
    return A_ub, b_ub


def slp_step(p):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None, float("inf")
    p = normalize_to_sum1(p)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 8 or n > 1536:
        return None, float("inf")

    c0 = convolve_full(p, p)
    active = get_active_indices(c0, n)
    if active.size == 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    try:
        A_ub, b_ub = build_slp_aub(p, active, c0)
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        cvec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cvec[n] = 1.0
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n + [(0.0, None)]
        res = linprog(
            cvec,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, float("inf")

    if res is None or not res.success:
        return None, float("inf")
    p1 = normalize_to_sum1(np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64))
    if p1 is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    cand, sc = best_alpha_line(p, p1)
    if cand is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None, float("inf")
    cur_sc = local_score_norm(p)
    if sc >= cur_sc - 1e-12:
        return None, float("inf")
    return cand, sc


def build_best_aub(p):
    n = int(p.size)
    R = 2 * n - 1
    A_ub = np.zeros((R, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
    for k in range(R):
        s = k - (n - 1)
        if s < 0:
            s = 0
        e = k
        if e >= n:
            e = n - 1
        if s <= e:
            start = k - s
            stop = k - e
            if stop == 0:
                A_ub[k, s:e + 1] = p[start::-1]
            else:
                A_ub[k, s:e + 1] = p[start:stop - 1:-1]
        A_ub[k, n] = -1.0
    b_ub = np.zeros(R, dtype=np.float64)
    return A_ub, b_ub


def best_response_step(p):
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None, float("inf")
    p = normalize_to_sum1(p)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    if n < 8 or n > 1024:
        return None, float("inf")

    try:
        A_ub, b_ub = build_best_aub(p)
        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
        cvec = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        cvec[n] = 1.0
        bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n + [(0.0, None)]
        res = linprog(
            cvec,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
        )
    except Exception:
        return None, float("inf")

    if res is None or not res.success:
        return None, float("inf")
    g = normalize_to_sum1(np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64))
    if g is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    cand, sc = best_alpha_line(p, g)
    if cand is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
        return None, float("inf")
    cur_sc = local_score_norm(p)
    if sc >= cur_sc - 1e-12:
        return None, float("inf")
    return cand, sc


def optimize_length(n, budget, seed, parent, initial):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 4 or budget <= 0.0:
        return None, float("inf")

    seq, sc = evaluate_normalized(initial)
    if seq is None:
        seq = make_central(n)
        sc = float("inf")
        if seq is not None:
            sc = local_score_norm(seq)
    if seq is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    best = seq
    bestsc = sc
    if not np.isfinite(bestsc):
        return best, bestsc

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + float(budget)
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    last_update = t0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 3.0:
            break

        improved = False
        lp_needed = 20.0 + 0.04 * n
        br_needed = 30.0 + 0.05 * n

        if _HAVE_SCIPY and n <= 1536 and rem > lp_needed and (it % 2 == 0):
            cand, sc = slp_step(best)
            if cand is not None:
                best, bestsc, imp = try_update(best, bestsc, cand)
                if imp:
                    improved = True

        if not improved and _HAVE_SCIPY and n <= 1024 and rem > br_needed and (it % 6 == 0):
            cand, sc = best_response_step(best)
            if cand is not None:
                best, bestsc, imp = try_update(best, bestsc, cand)
                if imp:
                    improved = True

        batch = 15.0 if n <= 768 else 8.0
        t_b = min(batch, max(1.0, 0.3 * rem))
        if t_b > 0.5:
            cand, sc = multiplicative_optimize_soft(best, t_b, int(seed) + it * 101)
            if cand is not None:
                best, bestsc, imp = try_update(best, bestsc, cand)
                if imp:
                    improved = True

        if improved:
            last_update = time.monotonic()
            stall = 0
        else:
            stall += 1

        if stall >= 4 and (time.monotonic() - last_update) > max(40.0, 0.15 * float(budget)):
            old_best = best
            old_sc = bestsc

            fresh1 = make_central(n)
            fresh2 = None
            if fresh1 is not None:
                fresh2 = fresh1[::-1]
            fresh3 = interpolate_parent_to_length(parent, n)

            for f in (fresh1, fresh2, fresh3):
                if f is None:
                    continue
                for beta in (0.20, 0.40, 0.60, 0.80):
                    cand = normalize_to_sum1((1.0 - beta) * old_best + beta * f)
                    if cand is None:
                        continue
                    best, bestsc, imp = try_update(best, bestsc, cand)
                    if imp:
                        last_update = time.monotonic()

            stall = 0

        it += 1

    return best, bestsc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1500, **kwargs):
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 1500.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 0.0:
        budget = 1500.0
    budget = max(20.0, min(2000.0, budget))
    deadline = start_time + budget - 10.0

    parent = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    parent_arr = None
    if parent is not None:
        try:
            parent_arr = np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)
            parent_arr = np.clip(parent_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if not np.isfinite(parent_arr).all() or parent_arr.size < 2:
                parent_arr = None
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    if parent_arr is not None:
        best_seq, best_score = evaluate_normalized(parent_arr)
        if best_seq is not None and np.isfinite(best_score):
            print("parent length={} score={:.7f}".format(best_seq.size, best_score), flush=True)
        else:
            best_seq = None
            best_score = float("inf")

    if budget >= 1200.0:
        lengths = [96, 128, 192, 256, 320, 384, 512, 640, 768, 896, 1024, 1280, 1536]
        max_chosen = 4
    elif budget >= 600.0:
        lengths = [128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 768, 1024, 1280]
        max_chosen = 4
    else:
        lengths = [96, 128, 192, 256, 384, 512, 768]
        max_chosen = 3

    if parent_arr is not None:
        p_len = int(parent_arr.size)
        if p_len not in lengths:
            lengths.append(p_len)

    length_best = {}

    for n in lengths:
        n = int(n)
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 30.0:
            break
        starts = make_starts(n, parent_arr, int(seed) + 17 * n)
        lb = None
        lsc = float("inf")
        for s in starts:
            seq, sc = evaluate_normalized(s)
            if seq is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc < lsc:
                lb = seq
                lsc = sc
        if lb is not None and np.isfinite(lsc):
            length_best[n] = (lb, lsc)
            best_seq, best_score, _ = try_update(best_seq, best_score, lb)
            if len(length_best) % 4 == 0 or n == int(lengths[-1]):
                print("quick length={} score={:.7f} global={:.7f}".format(n, lsc, best_score), flush=True)

    chosen = []
    items = []
    for n, val in length_best.items():
        items.append((val[1], n))
    items.sort()
    for _, n in items:
        if n not in chosen:
            chosen.append(n)
        if len(chosen) >= max_chosen:
            break

    parent_len = 0
    if parent_arr is not None:
        parent_len = int(parent_arr.size)
    if parent_len > 0:
        if parent_len not in chosen:
            chosen.insert(0, parent_len)
            if len(chosen) > max_chosen:
                chosen = chosen[:max_chosen]

    if not chosen:
        chosen = [512 if budget >= 400.0 else 256]

    weights = [1.0, 0.78, 0.60, 0.45][:len(chosen)]
    total_w = float(sum(weights))
    main_budget = max(20.0, min(deadline - time.monotonic(), budget - 20.0))

    used_w = 0.0
    for idx, n in enumerate(chosen):
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 30.0:
            break
        frac = weights[idx] / total_w
        alloc = main_budget * frac
        alloc = max(20.0, alloc)
        alloc = min(alloc, rem - 15.0)
        if alloc <= 5.0:
            alloc = max(10.0, (rem - 15.0) / max(1, len(chosen) - idx))
        if alloc <= 5.0:
            break

        initial = length_best.get(int(n), (None, float("inf")))[0]
        if initial is None:
            initial = interpolate_parent_to_length(parent_arr, int(n))
            if initial is None:
                initial = make_central(int(n))

        cand, sc = optimize_length(int(n), alloc, int(seed) + 1000 * (idx + 1), parent_arr, initial)
        if cand is not None:
            best_seq, best_score, imp = try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand)
            print("main length={} score={:.7f} global={:.7f}".format(n, sc, best_score), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if best_seq is not None and rem > 15.0:
        t = min(rem - 5.0, 120.0)
        cand, sc = multiplicative_optimize_soft(best_seq, t, int(seed) + 99913)
        if cand is not None:
            best_seq, best_score, imp = try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand)
            print("final multiplicative global={:.7f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if _HAVE_SCIPY and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 1024 and rem > 70.0:
        cand, sc = slp_step(best_seq)
        if cand is not None:
            best_seq, best_score, imp = try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand)
            print("final SLP global={:.7f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if _HAVE_SCIPY and best_seq is not None and best_seq.size <= 1024 and rem > 70.0:
        cand, sc = best_response_step(best_seq)
        if cand is not None:
            best_seq, best_score, imp = try_update(best_seq, best_score, cand)
            print("final BR global={:.7f}".format(best_score), flush=True)

    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = make_central(512)
    if best_seq is None:
        best_seq = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0

    final = normalize_to_sum1(best_seq)
    if final is None:
        final = np.ones(64, dtype=np.float64) / 64.0

    final_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(np.asarray(final, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)]

    try:
        official = float(evaluate_sequence(final_list))
        if np.isfinite(official):
            print("FINAL official={:.7f} length={}".format(official, len(final_list)), flush=True)
        else:
            raise ValueError("official inf")
    except Exception:
        if parent_arr is not None:
            fb, fs = evaluate_normalized(parent_arr)
            if fb is not None and np.isfinite(fs):
                final_list = [float(x) for x in np.clip(np.asarray(fb, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)]
                print("FINAL fallback parent={:.7f} length={}".format(fs, len(final_list)), flush=True)
        else:
            fs = local_score_norm(final)
            print("FINAL local={:.7f} length={}".format(fs, len(final_list)), flush=True)

    return final_list
```
